# Open threads register

> **Generated** from the escalation table (type=thread) by `Escalation.ps1 -Action Export -Type thread`. Do not edit by hand: respond through `Escalation.ps1 -Action Update -Id OT-nn` (escalation #2000). The hand-kept register (2026-10-02 to 2026-10-12) is frozen at `archive/open-threads-register-frozen-20261012.md`.

**Set up:** 2026-10-02 (researcher: *"I approve of the open threads register"*, #1935). Carried in the escalation table from 2026-10-12 (#2000).

**How it is used** (`cfg_behaviour_rule` open-threads-carried-as-escalations): The open threads register is carried in the escalation table as type='thread' (alias OT-nn), not edited as a markdown file. A thread is not worked as a task: it moves only when a strand reaches it (focused capture, #1900). Its items (escalation_thread_item; cluster, Strong's, verse, strand, chapter, event, researcher, escalation) are what a strand or task is matched against (Escalation.ps1 -Action Match). Claude manages each thread's lifecycle; the researcher gives feedback in chat, which Claude records verbatim the same turn, and there is no approval step (researcher 2026-10-12: 'the rest you need to control'). Every response to an item -- touched, narrowed, forward, ruling, note, closed, reopened, withdrawn, superseded -- is an escalation_thread_item_response row, recorded through Escalation.ps1 -Action Update -Id OT-nn -Item ... -ResponseKind ... -Respond ..., and written in full to the thread's history ('all interaction must go to history'). A thread completes when its last open item is settled; a thread is withdrawn or superseded (-SupersededBy OT-nn) with its open items settled in the same call. Notes and comments on a thread are Updates, never edits to the register file (rule T8). New held items and researcher questions are raised as threads at ledger approval. The file _analytics/cross-cluster-web/open-threads-register.md is generated (Escalation.ps1 -Action Export -Type thread) and regenerated at session-close.

**Threads:** completed 15, in-progress 1, on-hold 19, supersede 69 (104 in all). Item references are `OT-nn.n`.

| OT-99 · #2100 | **Fragmented verse readings across strands: an input for the segment re-read.** The M72 check of the completed strands (M25, M47, M18, M01, M02; 832 verses) found, in a test of 8, four readings that kept the strand's own clause and lost what the verse joins to it: 1Sa 7:3 (10.7 keeps "direct your heart", loses "he will deliver you out of the hand of the Philistines"); Mal 1:13 ("you snort at it", without "I am a great King", 1:14); Mic 4:4 (Ch 14 stops before "for the mouth of the Lord of hosts has spoken"); Jer 15:4 (FE-86 lists the people's deeds; the verse names "what Manasseh … king of Judah, did"). Researcher, verbatim: *"the triage of completed strands is pointing towards the existence of a deeper problem in the narrative - largely related to verse reading being fragmented accross different reading sessions … Downstream I will introduce a reread in any case of each segment of the narrative … it may be better to re-assess these complex context reading scenarios at that stage."* Inputs: `../Clusters/M72 - authority-dominion/m72-completed-strand-check-v1-20261012.csv` (832 rows, partner strands, narrative places) and `m72-completed-strand-check-test-v2-20261012.md`. | M72 completed-strand check (#1998, 2026-10-12) | the researcher's downstream re-read of each narrative segment starts | **on-hold**<br>OT-99.1 researcher the researcher's downstream re-read of each narrative segment starts -- open<br>OT-99.2 verse 1Sa 7:3 (named in the thread) -- open<br>OT-99.3 verse Mal 1:13 (named in the thread) -- open<br>OT-99.4 verse Mic 4:4 (named in the thread) -- open<br>OT-99.5 verse Jer 15:4 (named in the thread) -- open |
| OT-100 · #2101 | **The inner-being meaning of authority and ruling: not yet explored to the full.** The M72 group B reading (#1998) read 81 verses in 9 groups. The researcher's reading of it, verbatim: *"I don't think we really have discovered, debated or explored to the full the true meaning of authority and ruling from a inner being perspective.  M72 have added to the understanding, but maybe we are looking at it from the wrong direction … rule, authority, giving or having power is more of a state or status that works in collaboration with other characteristics and parties, but this is not yet strongly emerging from the material you are providing. I am not discounting that you are biased in your treatment of readings, so it is likely that I will need to read it again at some point."* Also: *"I dont' think the title of ch_11 is true to its contents"*. The current thoughts are in Ch 11 §7 *Authority as a standing* (v30), held as a first reading with a working note. Inputs: `../Clusters/M72 - authority-dominion/m72-group-B-reading-v1-20261012.md` (in it, the B word is often labelled the characteristic where another act carries the verse; see `m72-group-B-weave-record-v1-20261012.md` §2), the triage CSV, the passages file. | M72 group B weave (#1998, 2026-10-12) | the researcher re-reads the M72 material, or a later strand reads authority together with the characteristics that move inside it (fear, pride, wanting, care for another); the Ch 11 / §7 title question is answered | **on-hold**<br>OT-100.1 cluster M72 -- open<br>History before migration (from the register's status cell):<br>open; title answered 2026-10-12 (researcher: *"Ch 11 as a whole — the title should not carry any weight in the analysis, it is almost a collection of different inner being elements that does not have another home"*): Ch 11 renamed *Other sides of the inner being* (v31) and its opening restated. The meaning of authority stays open |

| OT-104 · #2145 | From the OT-02 die/death check (2026-10-14, check v2 section C, C24): in face D04 (struck down by God), these name an inner act and were not woven in Ch 2: Rev 2:23 (God 'searches mind and heart' as he strikes), 1Ch 10:14 (Saul 'did not seek guidance from the Lord. Therefore the Lord put him to death'), Eze 11:13 (Ezekiel falls on his face at Pelatiah's death: 'will you make a full end?'). Proposed homes: 13.1 for the first two, 10.9 for the third. |  | 13.1 or 10.9 is next read or widened | **on-hold**<br>OT-104.1 verse Rev 2:23 -- open<br>OT-104.2 verse 1Ch 10:14 -- open<br>OT-104.3 verse Eze 11:13 -- open |
